The 17 non-owning paths set `RootChanged=true`, so the planned conservative rule adds every workspace package and the external host. They include the root manifest and lockfile as well as shared or repository-level inputs: `.github/workflows/publish.yml`, `.opencode/skills/rtl-aware-development/SKILL.md`, `bun.lock`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, `infra/console.ts`, `nix/hashes.json`, `package.json`, `packages/sdk/openapi.json`, five `patches/*` files, `script/translate-app.md`, `script/translate-app.test.ts`, `script/translate-app.ts`, and `sdks/vscode/package.json`.

The public API predicate is true for the changed Protocol, Server, and OpenCode server paths. It explicitly includes Client, SDK, OpenCode, WebGUI, and the VS Code host. SDK and OpenCode were already direct owners. A no-root audit of the same reverse graph adds `@opencode-ai/sdk-next` as the recursive consumer of `@opencode-ai/core`. The root/shared expansion contributes the remaining non-direct packages `@opencode-ai/console-resource`, `@opencode-ai/httpapi-codegen`, `@opencode-ai/protocol`, `@opencode-ai/schema`, and `@opencode-ai/script`.

WebGUI has no direct changed path, but enters through the public API and server-behavior boundaries. Its watch-mode test uses gate `packages-opencode-webgui-test`; it has `build` but no `typecheck` script. `hosts/vscode-plugin` also has no direct changed path and is an external consumer, not a workspace package. Its manifest pins `pnpm@9.0.0+sha512.b4106707c7225b1748b61595953ccbebff97b54ad05d002aa3635f633b9c53cd666f7ce9b8bc44704f1fa048b9a49b55371ab2d9e9d667d1efe2ef1514bcd513`; gate `vscode-test` runs `pretest`, which runs compile and lint.

### Task 3 Gate IDs

### Task 3 Baseline Results

#### Approved Core Gate Resumption

- Formal Design/Spec decision: `a54e9b1b1b0f36aa0cc0b8816167c8e856f925b7` (`docs(opencode): specify low-concurrency core gate`).
- Formal SDK-next Design/Spec decision: `afb3c616276cbc0ee5b7b8c9fbccafd07f07f02f` (`docs(opencode): serialize sdk baseline gate`); plan commit: `8ddbe311b0983cde406d771bf1d4e5aaa09b71a1` (`docs(opencode): plan sdk serial gate`).
- Preflight at `28326739d2`: index empty, `MERGE_HEAD` absent, and all 65 protected fingerprints unchanged.
- The unique `packages-core-test` matrix command now runs the complete suite with the approved pinned `--max-concurrency=1` scheduling. Discovery Attempt 17 remains historical evidence; its current baseline Core result is reset to `pending` before the approved rerun.
- `packages-sdk-next-test` now uses `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun test --timeout 5000 --max-concurrency=1`. Its prior 5019.36ms failure remains recorded; three independent exact-command confirmations then passed with `5 pass | 0 fail | 19 expect()` in 5.53s, 7.07s, and 5.73s. No timeout or performance change was made.

#### Discovery Attempt 22 - OpenCode Temp-Directory Cleanup Stop

- `packages-opencode-test` ran with its exact package script, `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun run test`, after focused commit `5b0bcb9b60` (`fix(opencode): stabilize snapshot race test cleanup`). Its initial wrapper was interrupted at 10 minutes without a Bun exit; the subsequent complete run exited `1` after 1508.75s with `3551 pass | 58 skip | 1 todo | 1 fail`.
- The only failure was `test/session/snapshot-tool-race.test.ts`, which completed all four assertions but failed while `tmpdirScoped()` removed its already-empty temporary directory with Windows `EBUSY`. The focused package-script test passed once in 13.31s and then reproduced the same cleanup-only `EBUSY` on a later run. The removed `git fsmonitor--daemon stop` teardown was an unnecessary lock source because every git fixture configures `core.fsmonitor=false`; removing it did not eliminate full-suite contention.
- The evidence does not identify a repository behavior defect. The remaining options change test-execution policy or cleanup semantics: serialize this gate, expand its cleanup budget, or tolerate deferred cleanup. None is permitted by the current Task 3 constraints, so no timeout increase, skip, cleanup-error suppression, or additional retry was committed.

#### Discovery Attempt 23 - Approved Cleanup Retry Limit

- After the user-approved `maxRetries: 10` change, the exact focused command `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun run test test/session/snapshot-tool-race.test.ts` still exited `1` after 72.42s with `0 pass | 1 fail | 4 expect()`. The only failure remained `tmpdirScoped()` cleanup of an already-empty temporary directory with Windows `EBUSY`.
- `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun typecheck` exited `0`. The failed focused gate prevents the required source commit and the next full OpenCode gate. No process other than the diagnostic query referenced the failed temporary path after the test exited.

#### Discovery Attempt 24 - In-Process Lock Isolation

- Disabling `OPENCODE_EXPERIMENTAL_DISABLE_FILEWATCHER` before the Bun process started did not change the focused result: all four product assertions completed, then the same empty root directory failed cleanup with Windows `EBUSY`. The scoped watcher experiment was reverted without a source commit.
- A process-tree sample during cleanup showed only the Bun test process; its `bash` and Git children had exited. After excluding fsmonitor, the Parcel watcher, and a transient retry budget, the user approved preserving the snapshot race contract with the built-in `write` tool instead of the incidental shell resource boundary.

#### Discovery Attempt 25 - Tool Boundary Isolation

- Replacing `bash` with the built-in `write` tool preserved all four snapshot assertions but still ended with the same Windows `EBUSY`; the experiment was reverted. The tool implementation and child-process lifecycle are therefore not the lock owner.
- `provideTmpdirInstance()` currently registers temporary-directory cleanup inside a locally provided `InstanceStore` layer, so LIFO scope closure attempts directory removal before that layer's `disposeAll` finalizer. The fixture must explicitly dispose its own store entry when the test effect ends, matching the repository's CLI instance lifecycle contract, before `tmpdirScoped()` removes the directory.

#### Discovery Attempt 26 - Instance Store Disposal Ordering

- Explicitly disposing the local `InstanceStore` entry before `tmpdirScoped()` cleanup did not change the focused failure; the experiment was reverted. The lock is outside the local store entry lifecycle.
- The next diagnostic reduces the real fixture in stages instead of changing cleanup again: empty git-backed `provideTmpdirServer`, session creation, prompt loop, then summary diff. The first stage that reproduces `EBUSY` determines the owning subsystem.

#### Discovery Attempt 27 - File Existence Probe Isolation

- Real-fixture diagnostics passed for an unused git-backed server, session creation, prompt admission, and the full prompt-loop plus summary path. The full diagnostic without the extra file probe passed three consecutive reruns.
- Adding only `fs.access(race-test.txt)` made the same diagnostic fail cleanup with Windows `EBUSY` on all three reruns. The existence assertion is redundant because the completed write tool and non-empty snapshot diff already prove the mutation; remove that probe and its unused import rather than weakening cleanup.

#### Discovery Attempt 28 - OpenCode Full Gate Green

- `packages-opencode-test`: `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun run test` exited `0` with `3552 pass | 58 skip | 1 todo | 0 fail` across 3611 tests in 273 files (1767.82s). The suite reported 51 snapshots and 9514 expect() calls.

#### Discovery Attempt 29 - Storybook Bun Resolution And Heap Green

- The original `packages-storybook-build` exited `1` before Vite with `Could not detect Solid version: solid-js is not installed.` Storybook's Bun proxy delegates installed-package detection to `npm ls`; the inconsistent Bun `node_modules` tree makes that command exit nonzero, and Storybook therefore falls back to the declared `catalog:` specifier, which is not semver.
- Pinning Storybook's direct `solid-js` declaration to the unchanged catalog version `1.9.10` gives that fallback a valid semver. The next exact build reached Vite but exhausted Node's default 4 GiB heap. The package build script now invokes the existing Storybook dispatcher with `--max-old-space-size=8192`; the exact gate exited `0` after 14m39s. The mutable root install and the required frozen replay both exited `0` after the lockfile recorded the equivalent explicit version.

#### Discovery Attempt 30 - TUI POSIX Display Path Green

- `packages-tui-test` initially exited `1` with `190 pass | 1 skip | 1 fail`: on Windows, `abbreviateHome("/home/test/project", "/home/test")` produced `~\project` instead of the expected POSIX display path `~/project` because it used the host path separator.
- The helper now uses `path.posix` when both inputs are POSIX paths, leaving native Windows paths on the default host API. The focused regression test passed with `2 pass | 0 fail`, then the exact package gate passed with `191 pass | 1 skip | 0 fail` and `packages-tui-typecheck` exited `0`.

#### Discovery Attempt 31 - Remaining Matrix Green

- UI test, typecheck, and declaration build passed; the Web Astro build passed. Client generation completed with no generated-content diff. The full HTTP API exercise passed coverage, auth, and effect modes with `216 pass | 0 fail | 0 skip` in each mode.
- The VS Code consumer verified Corepack pnpm `9.0.0`, frozen install, compile, and test. Its test exited `0` with `228 passing | 1 pending`; Windows extraction lock diagnostics fell back to the bundled executable as designed.
- The Storybook manifest/lock commit triggered the mutable root install and frozen replay, both passing. No host manifest or pnpm lockfile changed, so the two host lockfile conditional gates are not applicable.

#### Discovery Attempt 17 - Core Gate Stop

- The prior `RepositoryCache > keeps branch checkouts isolated from branchless refreshes` signature passed when focused with its existing 30-second integration-test budget. A Git Trace2 run recorded 9.57 seconds of continuous Git work, with the two clones at 590.2 ms and 581.0 ms, feature/main pushes at 875.9 ms and 795.5 ms, no gap of 250 ms or more, and no remaining `git` process after exit. This rules out a lock, child-process, or resource leak for that signature, but does not justify adding another timeout.
- The focused `Npm.add > reifies when package cache directory exists without the package installed` test passed in 2.22 seconds. The current full `packages-core-test` gate then failed with `1091 pass | 7 skip | 1 fail | 0 error`: that same test timed out at 13140.16 ms under normal suite concurrency. This is a second unproven full-suite-only timeout class after eleven focused commits already expanded Core test budgets. No source change, timeout increase, skip, concurrency reduction, fix marker, or commit was made; Task 3 stops here for coordinator review.

#### Discovery Attempt 18 - Core Low-Concurrency Green

- `packages-core-test`: `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun test --only-failures --max-concurrency=1` from `packages/core` exited `0` with `1092 pass | 7 skip | 0 fail | 0 error | 0 todo` across 1099 tests in 143 files (323.39s). The skip count remains 7 and no todo was reported.

#### External CLI Runtime Recovery

The prior `packages-cli-build` failure, `Failed to extract executable for 'bun-linux-aarch64-v1.3.14'`, was isolated to Bun 1.3.14 cross-target runtime acquisition, not a repository source, manifest, lockfile, version, or target defect. Bun 1.3.14 source confirms the runtime comes from `@oven/bun-linux-aarch64@1.3.14` and uses `C:\Users\caiqy\.bun\install\cache\bun-linux-aarch64-v1.3.14`. A same-version package downloaded with `npm pack` was verified against its published SHA-512 integrity `X5Ss...wZw==`; its `package/bin/bun` was hard-linked into that one cache path. The original linux-arm64 compile probe was RED with the extraction failure; after prefill it was GREEN, bundling 2842 modules and compiling successfully. This environment-only recovery did not alter a repository path, package script, version, or compile target; `packages/cli/package.json` was verified clean after stat refresh.
The next CLI target, `bun-linux-x64-v1.3.14`, exposed the same absent-cache condition. Its minimal 2842-module compile probe was RED with the extraction error. `@oven/bun-linux-x64@1.3.14` was downloaded with `npm pack`, its tarball SHA-512 was verified against published integrity `sha512-7OVTAKvwfPmSbIV1HpdOoVVx5VRc427GuPPne93N6vk4eQBPId9nXmZDh9/zGaKPdbVjVtQSZafWQoUjx38Utw==`, and `package/bin/bun` was hard-linked to `C:\Users\caiqy\.bun\install\cache\bun-linux-x64-v1.3.14`. The same probe was then GREEN: 2842 modules bundled and `bun-linux-x64-v1.3.14` compiled. This was also an environment-only cache recovery with no repository change.

Further root-first replays confirmed the same missing-cache condition only as each CLI target was first reached. The verified cache recovery was repeated for `@oven/bun-linux-aarch64@1.3.14` (`sha512-X5SsPZHs+iYO8R/efIcRtc7gT2Q2DgPfliCxEkx4cXBumwkw0c/EsHMNwH3EgGpCDaZ7IYVPhpCG/xBOQHEwZw==`), `@oven/bun-linux-x64-baseline@1.3.14`, `@oven/bun-linux-aarch64-musl@1.3.14`, `@oven/bun-linux-x64-musl@1.3.14`, `@oven/bun-linux-x64-musl-baseline@1.3.14`, `@oven/bun-darwin-aarch64@1.3.14`, `@oven/bun-darwin-x64@1.3.14`, `@oven/bun-darwin-x64-baseline@1.3.14`, `@oven/bun-windows-aarch64@1.3.14`, and `@oven/bun-windows-x64-baseline@1.3.14`. For each, `npm pack` output was SHA-512 checked against `npm view ... dist.integrity`, then `package/bin/bun` (or `bun.exe`) was hard-linked to the exact versioned Bun cache name. The matching minimal compile probe was GREEN for every restored target, bundling 2842 modules for non-Windows targets and 2841 for Windows. Standard windows x64 needed no prefill. No operation changed a repository file, build script, package version, or compile target.

The single `gate-matrix` marker below is ordered execution state. Every gate object materializes `result`, `exit`, `pass`, `fail`, `error`, `skip`, `todo`, and `summary`, initially `pending` with null measurements. Task 3 updates the matching gate record, never a package aggregate: an executed gate becomes `passed`, `failed`, or `error`; an untriggered conditional becomes `not-applicable` with no test skip. No gate may remain `pending` after its condition is decided. No-script packages have no gate and no synthetic result.

The default matrix has 67 gates: one root frozen install, 60 workspace gates, two active public-API gates, and four VS Code host gates. It runs the root frozen install exactly once before package gates. The five conditional gates are `client-check-generated` after a generated change is committed, `root-regenerate-install` then `root-frozen-after-regenerate` whenever a Task 3 focused fix or later tag semantic handling changes a root/workspace manifest or `bun.lock`, and `vscode-lockfile-only-install` then `vscode-frozen-after-lockfile` whenever a Task 3 focused fix or later tag semantic handling changes the host manifest or `pnpm-lock.yaml`. No variable root install is part of the Task 3 baseline.

## Task 6 v1.18.8 Merge Evidence

- Merge commit `c6b302d017bf1125cbd766acd0090bbbfe34bd80` has first parent `966eaa7712fed408f6fb8cf776bec17479e216df` and second parent/tag `3c81a5d1ddceab377d9ad71c14899e6935333fdd`.
- User-approved resolution: `.gitattributes` adds `patches/*.patch whitespace=-space-before-tab`, scoped to patch artifacts only. It preserves `blank-at-eol` and all other whitespace checks. The MCP client patch remains byte-identical to upstream (`4205f158317d80f88dcb4d745dac46fa26af99bc`).
- `bun.lock` was regenerated by `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun install`. The public HttpApi generators completed from `packages/client` with `bun run generate` and from `packages/sdk/js` with `bun run build`; generated output has no worktree drift.

- Task 6 focused recovery: `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun test test/mcp/session-recovery.test.ts --timeout 60000` from `packages/opencode` exited `0` with `8 pass | 0 fail | 45 expect()`. Under Bun's default five-second budget, assertions completed before the occasional unnamed global-hook timeout while `AppRuntime.dispose()` ran; each of the two new single scenarios passed independently under that default budget. The scoped reviewer classifies this as a non-blocking cleanup-budget signal.
- Related HTTP lifecycle: the disconnect/reconnect and remote timeout cases exited `0` with `2 pass | 0 fail`; the full lifecycle file retains an unrelated Windows local-stdio fixture readiness failure, `stdio fixture did not publish its pid`, in `local stdio timeout terminates the real server process`.
- `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun typecheck` from `packages/opencode` exited `0`.
- The patch was generated in a clean Bun `1.3.14` workspace. A second clean workspace passed both pinned frozen applications, first with `--ignore-scripts` and then normally; no `.bun-tag-*` records are present. Final tracked patch SHA-256: `8BCE7DEF5B3B88BCA6CFB833DC026B09B76E921E295578CE6A8A88670D757CA5`.
- Final scoped reviewer: timeout-only, explicit signal, CJS/ESM, legacy handoff, failed-recovery cleanup, and single retry all pass. `Spec: PASS`; `Quality: PASS`; `Verdict: READY`. The post-`_commonHeaders()` guard has no `await` between it and `fetch`.

## Task 8 v1.18.9 Merge Investigation

- Preflight passed at verified v1.18.8 HEAD `53288faa0d3f66ab3bcf2fc831257e6fee68b58f`: bound branch `merge-upstream-tags`, branch isolation, empty index, absent `MERGE_HEAD`, and all 65 protected fingerprints unchanged.

- `Start-TagMerge` returned base `53288faa0d3f66ab3bcf2fc831257e6fee68b58f` and left `MERGE_HEAD` at the verified peeled commit. Git reported 33 unmerged paths: 27 release-version-only workspace manifests; `bun.lock`; `packages/console/app/package.json`; `packages/opencode/package.json`; the Task 6 MCP fixture and test; and the beta client patch.
- The 27 version-only manifests have no dependency, script, or other semantic difference after replacing their `version` with the tag value `1.18.9`. The remaining dependency and lockfile conflicts are coupled to the MCP replacement decision and are intentionally unresolved.

### Task 8 Resolution

- User selected the upstream legacy `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk@1.29.0` boundary and required every Task 6 recovery safeguard to be migrated into the Bun-generated legacy SDK patch. The beta client dependency and patch are removed.
- The legacy patch was regenerated through `bun patch --commit` and the merge carried blob `7325cbf2643fee535a5d9909bdf51ec26abb3892` (LF content SHA-256 `196FC0BA6C7E92861F7326A8D382303A86F69314782A674D7D980368C8A6C6DB`). It preserves CJS/ESM request-signal propagation, waiter cancellation during recovery and token preparation, post-header liveness checks, a one-retry limit, and recovery after a failed re-handshake. Review Fix Round 1/2 proved that blob's `index` target IDs stale and replaced it with the standard-generated blob `def7a0c2615e982e3770313164af5b95bbf2a449` (SHA-256 `59ECCAE343370F0FEBE5F03EB9EDC1EAD679E48231650478EE7B6425E153291B`); see the provenance section below.
- Pinned `bun install` regenerated `bun.lock` successfully. Pinned `packages/client` generate and `packages/sdk/js` build both exited 0; their generated changes are staged. The only SDK generated differences are output line-ending normalization.
- Focused verification under the package 30-second budget: `packages/opencode` recovery `8 pass / 0 fail / 45 expect`; MCP lifecycle `21 pass / 0 fail / 48 expect`. The direct Bun 5-second default timed out both an existing lifecycle test and a recovery fixture case, so it is not used as the gate.
- Task 9's full matrix has not started. This report remains unstaged and excluded from the Task 8 merge index.

### Task 8 Review Fix Round 1/2

- Dependency contract RED: at merge commit `8776a884be5a21dd116a634970acb11ad555cbdc`, an exact-tree assertion found both missing direct runtime dependencies: `hono`, `hono-openapi`. The pre-fix package test also failed at `dependencies.hono` with `Received: undefined`.
- Dependency contract GREEN: first parent `53288faa0d3f66ab3bcf2fc831257e6fee68b58f` declares both values as `catalog:`; current `packages/opencode/package.json` restores those exact values while retaining legacy `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk@1.29.0` and all resolved v1.18.9 dependencies. `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun install` regenerated `bun.lock`; it was not hand edited.
- Focused commit: `825bc54d5603463aaafc29cc6fa7f0c31f7a2613` (`fix(opencode): restore Hono runtime dependencies`) contains exactly `packages/opencode/package.json` and `bun.lock`. The report remains unstaged.
- Scoped gates at the focused commit: `packages/opencode` dependency test `1 pass / 0 fail / 4 expect`; `bun typecheck` exit `0`; session recovery `8 pass / 0 fail / 45 expect`; MCP lifecycle `21 pass / 0 fail / 48 expect`; root `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun install --frozen-lockfile` exit `0` (`2623 installs across 2927 packages`).
- Post-commit drift: from `packages/sdk/js`, `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun run build` exited `0`; `git diff --name-only -- packages/sdk/js` was empty. From `packages/client`, `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun run check:generated` exited `0`.

#### Legacy SDK Patch Provenance

- Root cause: the committed patch blob `7325cbf2643fee535a5d9909bdf51ec26abb3892` carries stale `index old..new` target blob IDs. Its four new IDs (`b768c8c0181c30cf5df0060a03df25237749554e`, `af7350bc93cc0ab40ae68385e0a44f79e56cba67`, `a1b79b873e17bb84353bc1f10035d20a83815840`, `82de3dfef8573abf91d94b2f557015f905707431`) do not match the LF files actually produced by applying its own hunks to the raw SDK (`7d717b01027a8264be3d9d0ad6835ae02ab67103`, `ab146296ff96cb7cd5e806606b1b96e253bf8f60`, `3f9a04c6ba25065a3f97c77861c5ecf896015f7d`, `daa2ba062a3d80ba4965e8b1f8f8e099b03f931f`). The old IDs all match (`a29a7d3a`, `3617e787f`, `624172aa`, `bfa2b7120`). The 667 non-index lines and all 32 hunk headers are byte-identical between committed and standard-generated patches; only those four `new` IDs differ.
- A clean standard generation in `C:\Users\caiqy\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\task-8-r2-final-generate` (detached `825bc54d56`): removed only `patchedDependencies["@modelcontextprotocol/sdk@1.29.0"]` from root `package.json`, ran `BUN_INSTALL_CACHE_DIR=C:\Users\caiqy\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\bun-cache-task-8-r2-final-generate vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun install --ignore-scripts` to produce an unpatched temporary lock while retaining the direct SDK dependency, ran `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun patch @modelcontextprotocol/sdk@1.29.0`, verified the raw prepared CJS transport contained neither `requestSignal` nor `_sessionRecoveryFailed`, applied the committed patch from its Git blob with `git -c core.autocrlf=false apply`, removed the target package's transient `.bun-tag-*` marker, then ran `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun patch --commit node_modules/@modelcontextprotocol/sdk`. No patch text was hand assembled.
- The standard-generated patch (`def7a0c2615e982e3770313164af5b95bbf2a449`, SHA-256 `59ECCAE343370F0FEBE5F03EB9EDC1EAD679E48231650478EE7B6425E153291B`, 679 LF lines, no `.bun-tag-*`) declares exactly the four actual target IDs `7d717b01/ab146296/3f9a04c6/daa2ba06`, matching the verified post-apply LF files. It replaces the stale-committed patch as `fix:v1.18.9:2`.
- Application commands: `BUN_INSTALL_CACHE_DIR=C:\Users\caiqy\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\bun-cache-task-8-r1-apply vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun install --frozen-lockfile --ignore-scripts`; then normal `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun install --frozen-lockfile`. Both exited `0`; the normal frozen run is the acceptance result. The installed CJS and ESM `streamableHttp.js` bundles each contain `requestSignal`, `isRequestActive`, and `_sessionRecoveryFailed`; their paired `shared/protocol.js` bundles each contain `requestSignal` and `isRequestActive`.
- Final HEAD verification at `8a07392380b743559be9a78d77f71e8131eb32c4` in second clean workspace `C:\Users\caiqy\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\task-8-r2-verify` (detached `8a07392380`): normal `vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun install --frozen-lockfile` exited `0` (after setting vfox Python for the native `tree-sitter-powershell` build; the first attempt without Python failed in node-gyp, an environment prerequisite, not a repository or patch defect). Installed CJS/ESM guard check passed 4/4; the workspace patch Git blob equals `def7a0c2615e982e3770313164af5b95bbf2a449`; patch and SDK contain no `.bun-tag-*` entries. Focused reruns: recovery `8 pass / 0 fail / 45 expect`; MCP lifecycle `21 pass / 0 fail / 48 expect`. `bun typecheck` exited `0` in the main repository where the gitignored `src/webgui/embed.generated.ts` build artifact exists; a fresh checkout must produce it via the opencode build before typecheck (pre-existing prerequisite, not changed by this fix). Root frozen install at the final HEAD exited `0`; post-commit drift none for `packages/sdk/js`, `packages/client/src/generated`, and `packages/client/src/generated-effect`; index empty and `MERGE_HEAD` absent.

#### Risk Signals

- The selected legacy `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk@1.29.0` boundary retains the migrated cancellation, liveness, failed-recovery, and one-retry protections in both CJS and ESM bundles; focused recovery and lifecycle gates characterize that behavior through the installed dependency.
- The standard-generated patch now matches the actual installed target blob IDs; any future SDK package-image or Bun patch-format change requires repeat byte-provenance plus CJS/ESM recovery verification before the patch may be updated.
- Fresh native dependency preparation may require a local C++ toolchain; `--ignore-scripts` was used only to prepare the isolated generation/application workspaces, while the required normal frozen application install passed.

## Task 9 v1.18.9 Gate Evidence

- Evidence commits: product/fix HEAD `74049e9bf795fbe0fe1f651c5546715912ea4705` (matrix executed at this tree); docs evidence commit `1451c831a00456a9713711b5a4e5e054f0cbb9b3` (report + tasks only, prematurely created by the implementer before review, accepted as process deviation without rollback); canonical testedHead remains `74049e9bf795` and subsequent docs-only commits do not change the product tree. Task 2.6 is ticked but still requires thorough review confirmation.
- Round record: merge `8776a884be5a21dd116a634970acb11ad555cbdc`, first parent/round base `53288faa0d3f66ab3bcf2fc831257e6fee68b58f`, tag parent `4da7bb44c84e013fa53e9c5d02ac753d1435c81a`, and final tested HEAD `74049e9bf795fbe0fe1f651c5546715912ea4705`.
- Rebuilt closure from `53288faa0d..74049e9bf79`: 93 changed paths; 28 direct owning workspace packages (app, cli, codemode, console/app, console/core, console/function, console/mail, console/support, core, desktop, effect-drizzle-sqlite, effect-sqlite-node, enterprise, function, http-recorder, llm, opencode, plugin, sdk/js, server, session-ui, slack, stats/app, stats/core, stats/server, tui, ui, web); root/shared unowned inputs (`package.json`, `bun.lock`, `patches/`, `nix/hashes.json`, workflows, docs) trigger `rootChanged=true` expansion, so the final closure is all 37 workspace packages plus external consumer `hosts/vscode-plugin`; `publicApi=true`. The root manifest/`bun.lock`, Protocol/Server/HttpApi paths, and generated SDK paths make the root regeneration, public API, and host default gates applicable.
- Final attempt executed 69 commands in matrix order from `74049e9bf795` (canonical v1.18.8 sequence reused because the closure is identical): 66 default gates (the root frozen default is replaced by `root-frozen-after-regenerate`), plus `root-regenerate-install`, `root-frozen-after-regenerate`, and `client-check-generated`. Every command exited `0`; no test recorded a nonzero fail/error or a skip/todo increase.
- The host manifest and `pnpm-lock.yaml` were unchanged in the round, so `vscode-lockfile-only-install` and `vscode-frozen-after-lockfile` are `not-applicable`, not test skips. Corepack matched the manifest pin `pnpm@9.0.0` and its complete SHA-512 pin.
- Final counters: App `724 pass | 0 fail` (690 unit + 34 browser); Client `16`; Codemode `263`; Console App `11`; Core `1092 pass | 7 skip`; Effect Drizzle SQLite `7`; Http Recorder `33`; HttpApi Codegen `66`; LLM `299 pass | 30 skip`; OpenCode `3577 pass | 58 skip | 1 todo`; WebGUI `158 files | 1451 pass`; SDK-next `5`; SDK JS `5`; Session UI `76`; TUI `191 pass | 1 skip`; UI `9`; HttpApi `216 pass | 0 fail | 0 skip | 0 missing | 0 extra`; VS Code `228 passing | 1 pending`.
- Focused fix `74049e9bf795fbe0fe1f651c5546715912ea4705` (`fix(opencode): restore v1.18.9 package gate`) contains only `packages/opencode/test/mcp/oauth-callback.test.ts`: the four `ensureRunning` tests replace fixed ports 18000/18001/18002/18003 with the file's existing dynamic `getFreeLoopbackPort()` helper because Windows Hyper-V's current reserved TCP excluded range `17932-18331` covers those fixed ports, making `listen` fail with WSAEACCES (reported by Bun as EADDRINUSE) while the connect-based `isPortInUse` probe returns false. No server behavior under test changes.
- Environment-only recovery: `packages/desktop/build` failed initially because the desktop prebuild downloads `@opencode-ai/cli-windows-x64-baseline@0.0.0-next-16350` and the user-level `.npmrc` registry mirror (`registry.npmmirror.com`) does not sync that nightly version (only placeholder `0.0.0`); the official npmjs registry has it. The gate reran with `NPM_CONFIG_REGISTRY=https://registry.npmjs.org/` set for the command only (no repository change); it then exited `0` and produced no repository diff. This is an environment prerequisite, not a repository defect.
- Final audits: Client generation and `check:generated` passed with no generated drift; legacy SDK build/test/typecheck passed; both `git diff --check 53288faa0d..HEAD` and working/index checks passed; index is empty, `MERGE_HEAD` is absent, and all 65 protected fingerprints match. After refresh, `packages/cli/package.json` and `packages/opencode/package.json` are absent from porcelain with no content or EOL drift.

## Task 10 v1.18.10 Merge Investigation

- Preflight passed at verified v1.18.9 docs HEAD `8238b77f7571baedea4301e890253bcb6e83103b`: bound branch `merge-upstream-tags`, branch isolation, empty index, absent `MERGE_HEAD`, and all 65 protected fingerprints unchanged.
- `opencode` remote tag `v1.18.10` was fetched and verified as lightweight: object and peeled commit are both `7902e04c3a67f7c69726bc955efb46e29214c797`.
- `Start-TagMerge` returned base `8238b77f7571baedea4301e890253bcb6e83103b` and left `MERGE_HEAD` at the verified peeled commit. Git reported 30 unmerged paths: `bun.lock` plus 29 package manifests (`sdks/vscode` is included this round).
- Upstream lineage: `v1.18.9` tag `4da7bb44c8` is NOT an ancestor of `v1.18.10` (`7902e04c3a`); the merge base of HEAD and the tag is `f28d72d15e00f51f7e30f9cbf08b810f292bb3ee`. The upstream `fix(mcp): restore legacy SDK compatibility` commit `982a9044c5` is an ancestor of both, so the MCP boundary and the legacy `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk@1.29.0` patch are untouched by this round and auto-merge cleanly; no equivalence candidate exists.
- Upstream `4da7bb44c8..7902e04c3a` changes 83 paths (1251 insertions, 359 deletions): toast-v2 overhaul with `solid-sonner@0.3.1` (catalog + `packages/ui` dependency), tab migration in app (`tab-migration.ts`, titlebar, settings/tabs tests, `--conditions=solid` on app test scripts), plugin modal model discovery (`packages/opencode/src/plugin/modal/*`), models URL default switch `models.dev` → `models.opencode.ai` (`packages/cli/script/generate.ts`, `packages/core/src/models-dev.ts`, `packages/stats/app/src/routes/model-catalog.ts`, `packages/ui/vite.config.ts`), `gitlab-ai-provider` 6.12.0 → 6.12.1, console Cloudflare `global_fetch_strictly_public`, `nix/hashes.json` nodeModules update, docs/content updates.
- No Protocol or Server HttpApi path changed in the upstream range, so `publicApi=false`: Client `bun run generate`, legacy SDK build, and `client-check-generated` are not applicable this round.
- Conflict resolution (no whole-file ours/theirs; version line only per manifest): all 29 manifests adopt the tag version `1.18.10`. `packages/console/app/package.json` retains the local `test` and `test:anthropic` scripts (same decision as v1.18.9). `packages/opencode/package.json` retains local `test:anthropic:replay` and the first-parent `catalog:` `hono`/`hono-openapi` dependency contract (Task 8 fix `825bc54d56` invariant), adopts `gitlab-ai-provider@6.12.1`, and keeps the legacy SDK patch unchanged.
- `bun.lock` was regenerated by pinned Bun (`vfox exec bun@1.3.14 nodejs@22.23.1 -- bun install`), converging after two regenerations; `bun install --frozen-lockfile` then exited `0` (`Checked 2624 installs across 2928 packages`). No lockfile lines were hand edited.
- Task 6/8/9 protections verified in the staged merge index: legacy SDK patch blob `def7a0c2615e982e3770313164af5b95bbf2a449` and `packages/opencode/test/mcp/oauth-callback.test.ts` are byte-identical to HEAD.
- Focused conflict/upstream-related verification (Task 10 scope only; no full matrix): opencode `test/plugin/modal-models.test.ts` `2 pass / 0 fail / 11 expect`; opencode `test/plugin` `168 pass / 0 fail / 516 expect`; app `src/context` `306 pass / 0 fail / 672 expect`; app browser `prompt-attachments` + `toast-owner` `10 pass / 0 fail / 19 expect`; UI `9 pass / 0 fail`; Session UI `76 pass / 0 fail`; Core models `12 pass / 0 fail / 30 expect`. Typecheck exit `0` for opencode, app, ui, session-ui, core, cli, llm, desktop, stats/app. Build exit `0` for app (vite) and ui (tsc). Root frozen install exit `0`.
- Merge index: 83 staged paths, zero unmerged paths, cached `git diff --check` clean, all 65 protected fingerprints unchanged. Only unstaged tracked path is protected `.gitignore`; this report is deliberately unstaged. Task 11's full matrix has not been started.
- Merge commit `a0f2f04bbb86fdfe987817672e363cfbd0ffdf6e` has subject `chore(opencode): merge upstream v1.18.10` and exact parents `8238b77f7571baedea4301e890253bcb6e83103b` / `7902e04c3a67f7c69726bc955efb46e29214c797`. Post-commit, `MERGE_HEAD` is absent, the index is empty, committed and working-tree diff checks pass, generated Client/Effect Client/legacy SDK paths have no drift, and all 65 protected fingerprints remain unchanged.
- Thorough review found one Important cross-lineage omission: upstream commit `a4f25a94b4` moved the v2 CLI model catalog default to `https://models.opencode.ai`, but the retained legacy OpenCode release generator still defaulted to `https://models.dev`; both `packages/opencode/script/build.ts` and `build-node.ts` embed that generator's snapshot. Focused follow-up `41e433dcc0c70bc06a490b0d2713a9361147a5f8` (`fix(opencode): align model catalog URL`) changes only that default. Its static assertion failed before the fix and passed after it; the `MODELS_DEV_API_JSON` import/parse path and pinned OpenCode typecheck also pass. Re-review is READY with no Critical or Important findings.
- Two remaining toast observations (`showPromiseToastV2` in Storybook prose and the app toast routing comment) are byte-identical in the verified second parent, so they are upstream pre-existing Minor issues rather than merge regressions and do not block Task 11. The canonical product HEAD entering Task 11 is `41e433dcc0c70bc06a490b0d2713a9361147a5f8`; the full matrix remains intentionally unrun.

## Task 11 v1.18.10 Gate Evidence

- Preflight at docs-only HEAD `dafec2ba89cdc53331a997a501f76bf5f17a1e7b` passed: product tree is equivalent to canonical candidate `41e433dcc0c70bc06a490b0d2713a9361147a5f8` under the product pathspec (`packages/**`, `hosts/**`, `package.json`, `bun.lock`, `pnpm-lock.yaml`), index empty, `MERGE_HEAD` absent, all 65 protected fingerprints unchanged.
- `Get-TagMergeRecord 'v1.18.10'` returned the single first-parent merge `a0f2f04bbb86fdfe987817672e363cfbd0ffdf6e` with first parent / round base `8238b77f7571baedea4301e890253bcb6e83103b` and second parent / peeled tag `7902e04c3a67f7c69726bc955efb46e29214c797`.
- Closure rebuilt from `8238b77f75..HEAD`: 88 changed paths; `rootChanged=true`; `publicApi=false`; 37 workspace packages plus external consumer `hosts/vscode-plugin`. The actual `Get-ImpactClosure` output is authoritative: root expansion applies `root-regenerate-install` and `root-frozen-after-regenerate`; `publicApi=false` leaves only the generation-bound gates not applicable. Not-applicable this round are exactly five conditionals: `client-generate` and `opencode-test-httpapi` (public-api condition), `client-check-generated` (post-generation-commit condition; no generation commits made this round), `vscode-lockfile-only-install` and `vscode-frozen-after-lockfile` (host manifest and `pnpm-lock.yaml` unchanged in the round). All other defaults — including the SDK JS (`packages-sdk-js-test`, `-typecheck`, `-build`) and WebGUI (`packages-opencode-webgui-test`, `-build`) gates — were executed and exited 0. Matrix total: 66 applied gates (60 package defaults after `root-frozen-install` replacement, 2 root conditionals, 4 host defaults) and 5 not-applicable.
- Attempt 1: first 33 gates passed (root regenerated `2624 installs across 2928 packages`, no changes; frozen identical). `packages-opencode-test` failed with exactly one failure: `test/tool/truncation.test.ts > Truncate > output > loads truncate effect in a fresh process` timed out at 20006ms against its inline 20000ms budget. The file is unchanged this round and the same test passes in isolation (`19 pass | 0 fail | 50 expect` in 7.16s) and under moderate parallel load (`99 pass | 14 skip | 0 fail` across 4 files in 42.63s); the 3578 other tests in the suite passed with 58 skip / 1 todo unchanged. This is a full-suite load-dependent fresh-process timeout: the time was spent somewhere in the fresh-process path under 274-file parallelism, and no process trace was performed to pin the exact stage. The inline budget is below the package test script's global `--timeout 30000`; Task 7 established the local-budget precedent for Windows integration cases, and the fix raises only the inline budget to the suite-consistent 30000ms. No behavior under test changes.

- Attempt 2 (final, fresh full matrix from the first gate): executed at product HEAD `5e6fc298854412ad4a7d52c8e681c1649eeb129b` (the focused fix commit; preflight was at the docs-only HEAD `dafec2ba89cdc53331a997a501f76bf5f17a1e7b`, product-tree equivalent to canonical candidate `41e433dcc0c70bc06a490b0d2713a9361147a5f8`). All 66 applied gates executed in matrix order and exited `0`. Root regenerated `2624 installs across 2928 packages` with no changes and the frozen rerun was identical. Test counters (fail/error 0 everywhere; skip/todo no increase over the v1.18.9 baseline): app `735 pass` (696 unit + 39 browser; baseline 724, upstream added tab/toast tests), client `16`, codemode `263`, console app `11`, core `1092 pass | 7 skip`, effect drizzle sqlite `7`, http recorder `33`, httpapi codegen `66`, llm `299 pass | 30 skip`, opencode `3579 pass | 58 skip | 1 todo` (baseline 3577; +2 upstream plugin modal tests), webgui `158 files | 1451 pass`, sdk next `5`, sdk js `5`, session ui `76`, tui `191 pass | 1 skip`, ui `9`, vscode `228 passing | 1 pending`. All 17 test-gate structured records carry numeric pass/fail/error/skip/todo counters derived from their per-gate logs; no null counters remain and the validation `unknown` list is empty. `packages-desktop-build` ran with the command-local `NPM_CONFIG_REGISTRY=https://registry.npmjs.org/` override recorded per the v1.18.9 precedent (nightly CLI baseline package not synced by the user-level mirror).
- Audits after the final attempt: no generated drift in `packages/client/src/generated`, `packages/client/src/generated-effect`, or `packages/sdk/js`; index empty; `MERGE_HEAD` absent; `git diff --check 8238b77f75..HEAD` and working-tree `git diff --check` clean; all 65 protected fingerprints unchanged; `packages/cli/package.json` and `packages/opencode/package.json` are absent from porcelain after stat refresh with no content or EOL drift (hash-object equals the HEAD blob).
- Final thorough review is READY with zero Critical, Important, or Minor findings. The first review required correcting the final `testedHead` from the pre-fix candidate to `5e6fc298854412ad4a7d52c8e681c1649eeb129b` and normalizing all 17 test-gate counters from their existing logs; scoped re-review confirmed the 66 records, marker JSON, timeout classification, product equivalence, and all closure conditions are consistent. No matrix rerun or additional product change was required.

## Task 12 v1.18.11 Merge Investigation

### Decision And Execution Evidence

- User decision: retain the downstream MCP superset and merge only upstream `c1ee3c6e34`'s SSE JSON-RPC error-response no-reconnect fix. The upstream patch was not taken wholesale.
- The resolved patch was reproduced without hand editing from raw `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk@1.29.0` in temporary detached worktree `C:\Users\caiqy\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\task-12-patch-provenance`: remove only its root `patchedDependencies` entry, run pinned Bun `install --ignore-scripts`, prepare with `bun patch --ignore-scripts @modelcontextprotocol/sdk@1.29.0`, apply the staged patch bytes, then run `bun patch --ignore-scripts --commit node_modules/@modelcontextprotocol/sdk`. Normal Bun installs materialize empty `.bun-tag-*` package metadata files, which are not part of the patch.
- The 32 conflict paths classify as 26 release-version-only workspace manifests, two protected-script manifests (`packages/console/app/package.json` and `packages/opencode/package.json`), one version-only `sdks/vscode/package.json`, `bun.lock`, `packages/core/src/models-dev.ts`, and the legacy SDK patch. The SDK files are the exact `merge-tree` three-way expected blobs, not a one-sided replacement: both non-ancestor lines changed them. No changed source path matches `packages/protocol/**`, `packages/server/src/**`, or `packages/opencode/src/server/**`; `packages/server/package.json` is version-only. Therefore `publicApi=false`; Client generation and the public-API-only HttpApi gate are not applicable. SDK JS test/typecheck/build was run as the generated-output consistency check.
- Task 6/8 protections remain present in CJS and ESM: `requestSignal`, `isRequestActive`, `_sessionRecoveryFailed`, one-retry recovery handling, and OAuth `determineScope`/`offline_access`. The additive upstream `isJSONRPCErrorResponse` received-response condition is present in both transports. Task 9 OAuth callback dynamic-port test, Task 10 catalog generator files, and Task 11 truncation budget file are byte-identical to the first parent.
- Pinned Bun `install` and `install --frozen-lockfile` both exited `0` (`2581 installs across 2874 packages`, no changes). ### Post-Merge Review Closure

- First focused fix `d94464e268` carried the source server key into notification session URLs. Review found two Important gaps: legacy layout redirects server-keyed URLs back to directory routes, and global errors have no session URL, so both could still inherit the previously active server.
- Second focused fix `75d6b6cca5` synchronously calls `server.setActive(sourceKey)` before every notification navigation. Its order regression passed `10 pass | 0 fail`; the broader route/context run passed `316`, the formal App gate passed `696 unit + 39 browser`, and App typecheck/build exited `0`.
- Scoped re-review of `d94464e268..75d6b6cca5` returned READY with zero Critical, Important, or Minor findings. It confirmed the legacy-layout and global-error findings are closed. The existing MCP SSE no-reconnect test's direct ESM-only coverage is unrelated to this App fix and remains a non-blocking residual note for the MCP test boundary.
- Task 12 is complete at product HEAD `75d6b6cca548317615932c1279e7aa1485d883ec`. Task 13's full matrix has not started.
